WHAT KHRUSHCHEV WAS SEEKING TO FIND OUT TODAY WAS HOW AMERICAN 
FARMERS ARE ABLE TO PROVIDE AN INCREASINGLY BETTER DIET FOR AN 
INCREASING POPULATION WITH FEWER AND FEWER PEOPLE ON THE FARMS. 

AMERICANS ARE DOING THIS WITH MECHANIZATION, FERTILIZERS, 
HERBICIDES, INSECTICIDES AND QUALITY FEED FOR LIVESTOCK, 

SOME OF THE PROCESSES WERE ON EXHIBIT TODAY, 

LINED UP AT THE HUGE CORNFIELD THAT EXTENDS A MILE IN EACH 
DIRECTION WERE ALL KINDS OF RED AND GREEN FARM MACHINES. INCLUDING 
ONE THAT PLANTS SIX ROWS OF CORN AT A TIME AND APPLIES BOTH 
FERTILIZER AND INSECTICIDES, TOO, 

GARST WAS TRYING TO IMPRESS ON HIS GUEST THAT THE FARM HAS BEEN 
SEEDED TO CORN FOR NEARLY A SCORE OF YEARS. GARST DOESN'T BELIFVF 
IN CROP ROTATION, BUT IN PUTTING THE NECESSARY NITROGEN BACK INTO 
THE GROUND THROUGH FERTILIZERS RATHER THAN CLOVER, WHICH DOES THE Jop 
MORE SLOWLY AND MORE EXPENSIVELY.

HERE AGAIN, GARST IS SOMETHING OF A PIONEER, IN CHOPPING UP CORN

AND A LONG FEED TROUGH. HE SHOWED THE PREMIER, TOO, HOW CORN IS 
CARRIED INTO BINS BY CONVEYORS.

THE KHRUSHCHEV MOTORCADE DROVE INTO THE MIDST OF A SCENE OF ENORMOUS 
CONFUSION AND ACTIVITY. 

SWARMS OF ARMY. AIR FORCE, POLICE AND OTHER SECURITY PEOPLE stoop 
GUARD ALL OVER THE FARM OF ROSWELL (BOB) GARST WHILE FEVERISH 
PREPARATIONS PROCEEDED FOR A BOUNTIFUL LUNCHEON UNDER A HUGE BROWN TENT 

* 

THOUSANDS OF PERSONS, INCLUDING SCHOOL CHILDREN, HAD LINED THE 
ROUTE ALONG HIGHWAY 141 FROM DES MOINES TOWARD COOW RAPIDS. THE 
CHILDREN WAVED AND CHEERED FOR THE BROADLY BEAMING COMMUNIST BOSS. 

THE SOVIET PREMIER GAVE NO SIGN THAT HE NOTICED A GRAY-HAIRED WOMAN 
STANDING AMONG THE CROWDS HOLDING A SMALL AMERICAN FLAG DRAPED IN 

EN ROUTE THE KHRUSHCHEV PARTY STOPPED AT ONE OF THE SEVERAL 
GARST FARMS ALONG THE HIGHWAY, WHERE WORKERS WERE BUSILY HARVESTING 
A CROP OF GRAIN SORGHUM. 

KHRUSHCHEV SHOWED KEEN INTEREST IN THE PROCEEDINGS. HE TRAMPED 
THROUGH THE FIELD WITH HIS HOST, ASKING GARST ALL SORTS OF QUESTIONS 
ABOUT THE CROP AND THE MACHINERY USED TO HARVEST IT. 

KHRUSHCHEV’S LOOK OF FATIGUE BEGAN TO DROP AWAY AND WAS REPLACED 
BY ONE OF EAGERNESS AS HE QUESTIONED GARST ABOUT THE METHODS HE 
USED TO FEED HOGS AND /(GHICKENS.

IN WASHINGTON OR ANY-OTHER POINT ON KHRUSHCHEV’S AMERICAN TOUR.

THURMONT -KERUSHCHEV 
BY MARVIN L. ARROWSMITH 

THURMONT, MD., SEPT. 23 (AP)-IN OWN OF ABOUT 2,000 POPULATION 
THE POLICE CHIEF IS A STRAPPING SIx- R NAMED JIM DUBELL. IN FACT, 
DUBELL 1§ THE ENTIRE POLICE FORCE--AND HE'S A MITE WORRIED. 

THE REASON HE'S WORRIED IS THAT NIKITA KHRUSHCHEV IS GOING TO BE 
IN THE NEIGHBORHOOD THIS WEEKEND. SOVIET PREMIER IS PLANNING TO 
SPEND A FEW DAYS WITH PRESIDENT EISENHOWER. 

THE PRESIDENT HAS A SECLUDED RETREAT, CAMP DAVID, UP THE ROAD A FEW 
MILES IN THE HEAVILY WOODED CATOCTIN MOUNTAINS, ALONG ABOUT FRIDAY 
EVENING EISENHOWER AND KHRUSHCHEV ARE GOING TO MEET AT THE CAMP AND STAY 
ON UNTIL SOMETIME SUNDAY, TALKING OVER THORNY WORLD PROBLEMS. 

THERE IS NOTHING NEW ABOUT HAVING EISENHOWER IN THE NEIGHBORHOOD. 

HE HAS BEEN DROPPING IN AT CAMP DAVID FROM TIME TO TIME SINCE 1953, 
AND HIS PRESENCE SCARCELY RIPPLES THE EVERYDAY ROUTINE IN THURMONT « 


THE PRESIDENT’S VISITS NEVER HAVE BEEN A SECURIT 

TOWN. SECRET SERVICE AGENTS WHO ACCOMPANY EISENHOWER FROM WASHINGTON, 
AND THE MARINE SENTINELS ON DUTY AT CAMP DAVID, ALWAYS HAVE THE 
SITUATION WELL IN HAND

IT WAS A BALMY NIGHT, NOT A CLOUD IN THE SKY, THE TEMPERATURE 
STOOD CLOSE TO THE 70 DEGREE MARK. 

SO IT WAS NOT THE WEATHER THAT INFLUENCED OFFICIALS TO USE A 
BLACK, CLOSED CAR INTO WHICH KHRUSHCHEV CLIMBED FOR THE DRIVE INTO 
THE City. 

THE DISCARDED OPEN CAR VAS A VERY SPECIAL ONE--DRIVEN HERE FROM 
NEW YORK FOR THIS OCCASION. IT IS THE SAME ONE PRINCESS BEATRIX OF 
THE WETHERLANDS USED LAST SUNDAY. 

THE ROUTE CHANGE TOOK THE PARTY INTO THE MOUNT WASHINGTON SECTION 
OF THE STEEL CITY--HIGH OVER THE PITTSBURGH POINT AREA WHERE THE 
ALLEGHENY AND MONONGAHELA RIVERS MERGE TO FORM THE OHIO. THIS GAVE 
KURUSHCHEV A BEAUTIFUL VIEW OF PITTSBURGH AT NIGHT. 

SKYSCRAPERS WERE LIGHTED AND THEIR LIGHTS REFLECTED FROM THE 
RIVER.

UNITED NATIONS, NeYey SEPT. 25 (AP)-DIPLOMATS SPECULATED TODAY 
THE UNITED STATES MIGHT HAVE HAD A HARD TIME GETTING THE U.N, TO 
BLOCK THE SEATING OF RED CHINA THIS YEAR HAD IT NOT BEEN FOR 
PEIPING'S RASH OF TROUBLE WITH HER NEIGHBORS. 

EVEN WITH THE WIDESPREAD VIOLENCE IN ASIA AND WESTERN CHARGES OF 
CHINESE COMMUNIST AGGRESSION, THE UNITED STATES EMERGED WITH ITS 
NARROWEST VICTORY SINCE THE isSUE FIRST CAME BEFORE THE ASSEMBLY 
NINE YEARS AGO.

RESOLUTION ASKING IT TO POSTPONE DISCUSSION OF PROPOSALS TO ADMIT 
THE CHINESE REDS OR EJECT THE CHINESE NATIONALISTS.

VICTORY MARGIN BY ONE MORE VOTE AS GUINEA, THE NEWEST U.N. MEMBER 
JOINED THE MANY ASIAN-AFRICAN MEMBERS SUPPORTING ADMISSION OF 


RED CHINA.

BRING IN RED CHINA, TOLD REPORTERS HIS SIDE WOULD HAVE PICKED 
UP MORE VOTES. BUT FOR INDIA’S CURRENT BORDER DISPUTE WITH 
THE CHINESE COMMUNISTS.

AFTER A@CUSING PEIPING OF GOADING WORTH VIET NAM INTO AGGRESSIVE 
ACTS ALONG HER NORTHERN FRONTIER.

THIS TIME WERE REPORTED TO HAVE CHANGED THEIR MINDS AFTER WORD CAME

UNITED NATIONS, NeYe, SEPT. 23 (AP)-PRESSURE MOUNTED HERE TODAY. 
ON THE MAJOR POWERS TO MAKE SOME KIND OF DRAMATIC MOVE TOWARD ~. _ 
PROGRESS IN DISARMAMENT. 


REPRESENTATIVES OF THE SMALLER NATIONS MADE PLAIN IN POLICY SPEECHES 


TO THE Se ontion ASSEMBLY THAT THEY REGARD DISARMAMENT AS A TOP ISSUE 
E ° 

, IRELAND CALLED ON THE UNITED STATES, BRITAIN AND THE SOVIET UNION 
TO START THE BALL ROLLING BY AGREEING hoT TO GIVE THEIR WEAPONS TO 
NON-NUCLEAR POWERS. THE NON-NUCLEAR POWERS, IN TURN, WOULD AGREE 
NOT TO MAKE OR ACCEPT NUCLEAR WEAPONS. 
IRISH FOREIGN MINISTER FRANK AIKEN ALSO URGED THE NUCLEAR 
POWERS TO ENCOURAGE GROUPS OF NATIONS “TO ACCEPT THE RULE OF LAW, AREA 
BY AREA, THROUGHOUT THE WORLD." HE ADDED: 

"THEY SHOULD ALSO BE PREPARED TO SUPPORT A PERMANENT U.N. 
FORCE DESIGNED TO PROTECT ONE SUCH AREA FOR A BEGINNING. IN THIS WAY 
THEY WOULD GIVE THE WORLD CONCRETE EVIDENCE OF THEIR DETERMINATION 
TO UPHOLD THEIR CHARTER PLEDGES, AND THEIR DETERMINATION TO BUILD A 
WORLD ORDER BASED ON JUSTICE AND LAW, AND COOPERATIVELY DEFENDED 
BY A COMMON FORCE.® 


wae THE PLAN BE APPLIED TO THE PROBLEM OF BERLIN AND
